def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  for _ in range(100):
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12: break
    if s<target:
       need=target-s
       room=(1-h); sroom=room.sum()
       if sroom==0: break
       # scale room by theta? water fill: add diff uniformly at room? Previous adds diff/total*room, one step might hit bound; iterate.
       h = np.clip(h + need/room_sum*room, 0,1)
    else:
       h = np.clip(h * (target/s), 0,1) # scaling down may not enough, repeat
  # final linear fix for tiny diff among non-bound
  if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-13:
     idx=(h<1)&(h>0) maybe
...
  return h
```

`project_feasible(y,n)` using bisection? We'll need many calls. Use efficient iterative but robust:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    target=n/2
    h=np.clip(y,0,1)
    # if sum close return
    for _ in range(50):
        s=h.sum()
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
           room=1-h; sr=room.sum()
           if sr<1e-15: break
           h=np.clip(h+diff/sr*room,0,1)
        else:
           s=h.sum()
           if s<1e-15: break
           h=np.clip(h*target/s,0,1)
    # final residual
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1-h)
          h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
       else:
          idx=np.argmin(h)
          h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
If clipping residual into one idx may still not exact if idx saturated; loop. Good.

Block/ADAM smoother:
```
def lse_weighted_grad(c, h, n, alpha=None, topk=None):
   if c.max()==0: return 0
   m=c.max()
   if alpha is None: w = np.zeros(len(c)); w[c.argmax()]=1
   else:
      shifted=alpha*(c-m); shifted-=shifted.max(); w=np.exp(shifted); w/=w.sum(); 
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   active = np.nonzero(w>1e-12)[0]
   for k_idx in active:
      wk=w[k_idx]
      if wk==0: continue
      k = k_idx - (n-1)
      # forward derivative
      if k>=0:
         # for i=0..n-1-k: h_i(1-h_{i+k})
         # grad i += wk*(1-h_{i+k}) for i<n-k
         grad[:n-k] += wk*(1-h[k:])
         # backward from negative? For this k, also terms i? For c_k with k positive, derivative wrt h_j from j as second index: -h_{j-k} for j>=k.
         grad[k:] -= wk*h[:-k]
      else:
         kk=-k
         # c_{-kk}=sum_i h_i(1-h_{i-kl})
         grad[kk:] += wk*(1-h[:-kk])? Wait first index i, second i-kk. derivative wrt first: (1-h_{i-kk}) for i>=kk.
         grad[kk:] += wk*(1-h[:-kk])
         # derivative wrt second: -h_i for i< n-kk
         grad[:n-kk] -= wk*h[n-kk:]
      if k==0:
         # above double? For k=0, forward grad[:]+=1-h, backward grad-=h; sum =1-2h, good.
   return dx*grad
```

Weighted_grad_from_counts: Avoid loops over all 2n-1 with weights if many active. If topk small, loop over topk only. For softmax high maybe many; use topk e.g. 32. Good.

```
def weighted_grad_counts(c,h,n,w_idx=None,weights=None):
   dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n)
   if w_idx is None:
      w_idx=np.arange(len(c)); weights=np.full(len(c),1.0/len(c))? no, use all normalized? Slow.
   for idx,w in zip(w_idx,weights):
      if abs(w)<1e-14: continue
      tau=idx-(n-1)
      if tau>=0:
         if tau==0:
            grad += w*(1-2*h)
         else:
            grad[tau:] += w*(1-h[:-tau])
            grad[:n-tau] -= w*h[:-tau]
      else:
         s=-tau
         grad[:n-s] += w*(1-h[s:])
         grad[s:] -= w*h[:-s]
   return grad*dx
```

For negative tau: c = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1-s} h_j (1-h_{j+s}); first grad[:n-s]+= (1-h[s:]); second derivative: term -h_j for p=j+s, j=p-s: grad[s:] -= h[:-s]. Good.

def make_candidates(h, n, rng, num):
  cands=[]
  # small Gaussian
  for amp in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
      g=rng.normal(size=n)*amp; g -= g.mean(); cands.append(project(h+g))
  # low freq sinusoids
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  for m in [1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16]:
      a=rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3); b=rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      f=a*np.cos(2πm*x/2+phase)+b*np.sin(...)
      f-=f.mean(); cands.append(project(h+scale*f))
  # random pairs scaling? complement?

Another promising technique: use gradient of max to do local line search over direction d. At current h, compute subgradient g of active max (weighted). Move h along -g projected, with line search on C. Because objective nonconvex, can do Armijo: step sizes reduce until C improves. This is cheap (few gradient evals) and can reduce local maximum. Let's implement `gradient_line_search(h,n,deadline)`:
- Compute c, active top weights, grad weighted (dx included maybe not). Use active max gradient normalized.
- For each base lr in [1e-3,5e-3,1e-2,5e-2,0.1,0.2], try h_new=project(h - lr*grad) and maybe h +? (since we want reduce max, negative gradient of weighted objective). Compute C. Accept best. Then repeat until no improvement. Also try negative? The gradient of max points increase; -grad descent.
- Could use Adam for many steps.
This is similar to subgradient but line search ensures descent if possible.

Let's design `active_gradient(c,h,n,topk=16,alpha=None)`:
- if topk: idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:] (for length). If c max zeros? weights=1/len.
- weights: let csel; `w = np.exp(gamma*(csel-csel.max()))`; gamma maybe 50? c values counts; gap maybe small. Use `gamma = 20.0 / (csel.max()-csel.min()+1e-8)`? If all equal, uniform. But topk already. Maybe use `w = 1.0 + np.maximum(0, csel - csel.max()+ (csel.max()-csel.min()))?` Simpler uniform topk. Subgradient of max doesn't need smoothness; uniform topk is averaging active. Use topk adaptive (2,4,8,16).
- Compute grad via weighted_grad_counts.
- Normalize? grad magnitude O(dx*n?) For top active, grad count ~ n*dx=2? So O(1). LR in h units.

Implement `subgrad_line_search(h,n,max_iter,deadline,lrs)` with active topk=8. Each iteration:
```
c=corr; cmax=c.max()*dx
g = activeGradient(c,h,n, topk=8)
g = g - np.mean(g) # to not violate sum? projection handles but helps
best = h,cmax
for lr in lrs:
  hn=project(h - lr*g,n)
  cn=compute_c5(hn,n)

```

Pair generation:
```
def active_pair_list(h,n,c,rng,top_m=4,top_terms=8):
  m_idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_m)[-top_m:]
  pairs=set()
  for mi in m_idx:
    tau=mi-(n-1)
    if tau>=0:
       js=np.arange(tau,n); ts=js-tau
    else:
       js=np.arange(0,n+tau); ts=js-tau
    vals=h[js]*(1-h[ts])
    # top term indices
    if len(vals)>top_terms:
       top=np.argpartition(vals,-top_terms)[-top_terms:]
    else: top=...
    for idx in top:
       a=int(js[idx]); b=int(ts[idx]); pairs.add((min(a,b),max(a,b)))
    # also maybe adjacent coordinate pair: a,b from high vals
  # random pairs
  for _ in range(20): p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n); if p!=q add
  return list(pairs)
```

def try_pairs(h,pairs,n,grid,deadline,current_c):
  for it in range(10):
    if time>deadline: break
    improved=False
    for p,q in pairs:
      if p==q: continue
      new_h, new_c = try_pair(h,p,q,n,grid,current_c)
      if new_c < current_c - 1e-12:
         h=new_h; current_c=new_c; improved=True; pairs=active_pair_list? maybe update after sweep
    if not improved: break
    # optionally update pairs to active
  return h,current_c
```

```
def try_pair(h,p,q,n,grid,current_c):
  hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; s=hp+hq; tlo...; if width small return h,current_c
  ts=np.linspace(tlo,t_hi,grid)
  # include hp
  ts=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts,[hp, (tlo+t_hi)/2])))
  best_t=hp; best_u=hq; best=c current maybe
  for t in ts:
    u=s-t
    if u<0 or u>1: continue
    h[p]=t; h[q]=u
    cnt = int(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h,'full')))
    if cnt < best:
       best=cnt; best_t=t; best_u=u
  h[p]=best_t; h[q]=best_u
  enforce_sum(h,n) # may alter slight
  # final cnt after enforce? recompute if changed
  if best < current_c:
      # enforce may change; recompute
      final_cnt=np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))
      if final_cnt < current_c:
         return h,final_cnt
  else:
      # restore original
      h[p]=hp; h[q]=hq
  return h,current_c
```

```
def feasible(T, c_nd, a_nd, h0, diag_c, diag_base, unaff_max):
 if unaff_max > T+eps: return False,None
 lo=0; hi=1
 if len(a_nd):
   pos=a_nd>eps; neg=a_nd<-eps
   if any pos: hi=min(hi, np.min(h0 + (T-c_nd[pos])/a_nd[pos]))
   if any neg: lo=max(lo, np.max(h0 + (T-c_nd[neg])/a_nd[neg]))
 if diagonal:
   D = T - diag_c + h0*(1-h0) # should this? c0_new = diag_c + (x(1-x)-h0(1-h0)) <=T => x(1-x) <= T - diag_c + h0(1-h0). yes.
   if D < -eps: infeasible
   elif D < 0.25 - eps:

def coord_feasible(T, c_nd, a_nd, zero_max, h0, c_diag, d_base, unaff):
  if unaff > T+1e-14: return False,0,0
  xlo=0; xhi=1
  if lenpos:
     pos=a_nd>eps; hi=np.min(h0+(T-c_nd[pos])/a_nd[pos]); xhi=min(xhi,hi)
  if lenneg:
     neg=a_nd<-eps; lo=np.max(h0+(T-c_nd[neg])/a_nd[neg]); xlo=max(xlo,lo)
  if xlo > xhi+1e-14: return False
  if np.any(zero): if c_nd_zero_max > T+eps return False
  if has_diag:
     D = T - c_diag + d_base # d_base=h0*(1-h0)
     if D < -1e-14: return False
     if D < 0.25 - 1e-14:
        r1=(1-sqrt(1-4D))/2; r2=...
        # interval [xlo,xhi] after lines
        if xhi <= r1 or xlo >= r2: pass (entire interval feasible)
        else:
           left_len = min(xhi,r1)-xlo if xlo<r1 else -
           right_len = xhi-max(xlo,r2) if xhi>r2 else -
           # feasibility if any

           if left_len > -1e-14 or right_len > -1e-14:

              else: xlo=max(xlo,r2)
              # after choice, if interval invalid maybe infeasible
              if xlo > xhi+1e-14: return False
          else return False
  return True,xlo,xhi

```
def project_feasible(y,n):
  y=np.asarray(y,float)
  target=n/2
  # clip to [0,1]
  h=np.clip(y,0,1)
  # if sum out, adjust by adding scalar to h (with clipping)
  for _ in range(80):
     s=h.sum()
     diff=target-s
     if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
     h=np.clip(h+diff,0,1)  # add constant to all; may saturate
  # final residual into index
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
     if diff>0:
        idx=np.argmax(1-h)
        h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
     else:
        idx=np.argmin(h)
        h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
  # if still (idx saturated), scale non-bound
  if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-12:
     idx=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
     if np.any(idx):
        avg? We need adjust values within bounds to target while fixing others? Solve waterfilling:
           h=np.clip(y+lambda) bisection.
```
Simpler exact projection: bisection on λ for y (not necessarily clipped). Since feasible set {0≤x≤1, sum target}. Projection in Euclidean? Not required; any feasible close works. We can do:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
  target=n/2
  # Find λ such that sum clip(y+λ,0,1)=target.
  lo=y.min()-1.0; hi=y.max()+1.0
  for _ in range(60):
    mid=(lo+hi)/2
    s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum()
    if s>target: hi=mid
    else: lo=mid
  h=np.clip(y+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
  # final adjust residual in one index
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
     idx=np.argmax(1-h) if diff>0 else np.argmin(h)
     h[idx]+=diff
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This is simpler and exact. But if y has large values, bisection 60*n maybe. For n=700, 42k per call. If 1000 calls, 42M, ok. But within ADAM many calls, 1000*42k=42M, fine. For n=1000 60M. Good. However, bisection uses y not clipped; if y sum far, works. It preserves relative y. For coordinate projection after pass, y=h values near feasible; λ small. Good. Use this. Final diff adjust may break bounds if diff too large; but bisection should get close. Use loop a few.

def enforce_sum(h,n):

 if abs(diff)<1e-13: return h

def try_pairs(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=None,grid=33,rng=None):
  count = float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')))
  best_count=count
  sweeps=0
  while time<deadline and (max_sweeps is None or sweeps<max_sweeps):
     pairs=active_pair_list(h,n,np.correlate? we need c for top shifts? Could pass count but need c. Compute c once per sweep.
     c=np.correlate(...)
     if c.max() < best_count-1e-12: best...
     pairs=active_pair_list_from_c(h,n,c,rng)
     improved=False
     for p,q in pairs:
       p,q=int(p),int(q)
       if p==q: continue
       improved_pair, count = try_pair_inplace(h,p,q,n,grid,count)
       if improved_pair:
          improved=True
          if count < best_count: best_count=count
     sweeps++
     if not improved: break
  return h,best_count
```
Need avoid computing c for active twice. In try_pair_inplace, if no improvement, count unchanged. We can recompute count at start of next sweep. Good.

`active_pair_list_from_c`: uses c to choose active shifts. For top m, choose terms. Need term values for selected m. Could use c array and h.
```
def active_pair_list_from_c(h,n,c,rng):
  m_idx=np.argpartition(c, -min(5,len(c)))[-5:]
  pairs=set()
  for mi in m_idx:
    tau=int(mi-(n-1))
    if tau>=0: js=np.arange(tau,n); ts=js-tau
    else: js=np.arange(0,n+tau); ts=js-tau
    if len(js)==0: continue
    vals=h[js]*(1-h[ts])
    k=8
    if len(vals)>k: top=np.argpartition(vals,-k)[-k:]
    else: top=np.arange(len(vals))
    for ii in top:
       a=int(js[ii]); b=int(ts[ii])
       if a!=b: pairs.add((min(a,b),max(a,b)))
       else:
          # diagonal: pair this with a random/median other
          if rng is not None: q=rng.randint(n)
          else: q=(a+1)%n
          if a!=q: pairs.add((min(a,q),max(a,q)))
  # random pairs
  for _ in range(25):
     p=rng.randint(n) if rng else int((len(pairs)+1)%n)
     q=rng.randint(n) if rng else int((p+7)%n)
     if p!=q: pairs.add((min(p,q),max(p,q)))
  return list(pairs)
```
Use rng; for deterministic when rng None? We'll pass rng.

def try_pair_inplace(h,p,q,n,grid,current_count):
  hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; s=hp+hq
  tlo=max(0.0,s-1.0); thi=min(1.0,s)
  if thi-tlo < 1e-10 or p==q: return False,current_count
  base = hp
  ts = np.linspace(tlo,thi,grid)
  # add base and mid
  ts = np.r_[ts, base, 0.5*(tlo+thi)]
  ts = ts[(ts>=tlo-1e-12)&(ts<=thi+1e-12)]
  ts = np.clip(ts,tlo,thi)
  if len(ts)>60: ts_unique = np.unique(np.round(ts,12))? Use np.unique(ts)
  best_t=hp; best_u=hq; best=current_count
  # To avoid restore if no improve, store hp/hq and set at end.
  for t in ts:
    t=float(t); u=s-t
    if u< -1e-12 or u>1+1e-12: continue
    # if p/q order, h[p]=t, h[q]=u; if q chosen as other but s same; ok
    h[p]=t; h[q]=u
    cnt=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
    if cnt < best - 1e-15:
      best=cnt; best_t=t; best_u=u
  # refine around best index? We need index; track best_pos
  if best < current_count - 1e-14:
     # refine local if best_t not endpoint
     lo = tlo if best_t <= ts[0]+1e-12 else max(tlo, best_t - (thi-tlo)/grid)
     hi = thi if best_t >= ts[-1]-1e-12 else min(thi, best_t + (thi-tlo)/grid)
     ts2=np.linspace(lo,hi,max(15, grid//3))
     for t in ts2: ...
     h[p]=best_t; h[q]=s-best_t
     return True,(float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))))
  else:
     h[p]=hp; h[q]=hq
     return False,current_count
```

Now random/low-dim trial generation. We need generate diverse h. Parent initial may be local. We can create:

Coordinate descent with corrected solve: Need code length. Let's write top-level:
```
def solve_coord_correct(c,h,p,n,nbisect=30):
    h0=float(h[p])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-14: return h0
    lo_m = max(0, n-1-p); hi_m = min(2*n-2, n-1+p)
    ms = np.arange(lo_m, hi_m+1)
    tau = ms - (n-1)
    jf = p + tau; jb = p - tau
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += (1.0 - h[jf[vf]])
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    diag = ms == (n-1)
    has_diag = bool(np.any(diag))
    if has_diag:
       di=int(np.flatnonzero(diag)[0])
       c_diag=float(c_aff[di])
       a[di]=0.0
    else: c_diag=-1.0
    non = ~diag if has_diag else np.ones(len(ms),bool)
    c_nd = c_aff[non]
    a_nd = a[non]
    zero_max = -1.0
    e=1e-13
    if len(c_nd):
       zmask=np.abs(a_nd)<e
       if np.any(zmask): zero_max=float(np.max(c_nd[zmask]))
       pos_idx=np.flatnonzero(a_nd>e)
       neg_idx=np.flatnonzero(a_nd<-e)
    else: pos_idx=neg_idx=np.empty(0,int); zero_max=-1
    unaff=0.0
    if lo_m>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[:lo_m])))
    if hi_m<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[hi_m+1:])))
    cur_max=float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= cur_max - 1e-12: return h0
    base_diag = h0*(1-h0)
    Llo=float(unaff); Lhi=float(cur_max)
    # feasibility nested? closures not allowed? We can define top-level helper? But needs many local arrays; closure not allowed? Rule: no closures or lambdas. We can implement loop inline with duplicated code or define top-level `coord_feasible` taking all arrays. But arrays as args; OK. Top-level function can be called. It may be slower due function calls 35*700. Accept.
```

`coord_feasible`:
```
def coord_feasible(T, unaff, pos_a,pos_c,neg_a,neg_c,zero_max,h0,c_diag,base_diag,has_diag):
  if T < unaff - 1e-14: return False,0,0
  xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
  eps=1e-14
  if pos_a.size:
     b = np.min(h0 + (T - pos_c)/pos_a)
     if b < xhi: xhi = float(b)
  if neg_a.size:
     b = np.max(h0 + (T - neg_c)/neg_a)  # neg_a negative; division yields negative for T-c? But formula h0+(T-c)/a works.
     if b > xlo: xlo = float(b)
  if zero_max > T + eps: return False,0,0
  if xlo > xhi + eps: return False,0,0
  if has_diag:
     D = T - c_diag + base_diag
     if D < -eps: return False,0,0
     if D < 0.25 - eps:
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0*D
        if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
        rt=np.sqrt(disc)
        r1=(1.0-rt)*0.5; r2=(1.0+rt)*0.5
        if not (xhi <= r1 + eps or xlo >= r2 - eps):
           left = min(xhi, r1) - xlo
           right = xhi - max(xlo, r2)
           if left <= -eps and right <= -eps:
              return False,0,0
           # choose side
           if xlo < r1 and xhi > r2:
              # choose side containing h0 if possible else longer
              if h0 <= r1 and left > -eps:
                 xhi = min(xhi, r1)
              elif h0 >= r2 and right > -eps:
                 xlo = max(xlo, r2)
              elif left >= right:
                 xhi = min(xhi, r1)
              else:
                 xlo = max(xlo, r2)
           elif xlo < r1:
              xhi=min(xhi,r1)
           else:
              xlo=max(xlo,r2)
           if xlo > xhi + eps:
              # Maybe choose other side
              if left > -eps: xhi=min(xhi,r1); xlo=min(xlo,r1)
              elif right > -eps: xlo=max(xlo,r2); xhi=max(xhi,r2)
              else: return False,0,0
  # clamp
  if xlo <0: xlo=0
  if xhi>1: xhi=1
  if xlo > xhi+1e-12: return False,0,0
  return True, xlo, xhi
```

For right: xlo=max(xlo,r2).

Now `coord_descent`:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,random_order=True,nbisect=30):
  h=h.copy(); c=corr_counts(h)
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>=deadline: break
    improved=False
    if random_order and rng is not None:
       order=rng.permutation(n)
    else: order=np.arange(n)
    for p in order:
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       h0=float(h[p])
       if h0<=1e-14 or h0>=1-1e-14: continue
       x=coord_step(c,h,p,n,nbisect)
       if abs(x-h0)>1e-12:
          h[p]=x; improved=True
    if not improved: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=corr_counts(h)
  h=enforce_sum(h,n)
  return h, compute_c5(h,n)
```
If projection changes h, c recomputed. If projection negates improvement but next pass maybe no improved. OK.

Potential performance: `coord_step` creates `ms` arange each p. Could precompute ranges? Not necessary.

`corr_counts` can use `np.correlate`. To avoid repeated allocation of 1-h, we can compute `1-h` each. OK.

def sine_component(n, rng, freqs):
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  f=np.zeros(n)
  for m in freqs:
    amp=rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1)
    phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    f += amp*np.sin(np.pi*m*x+phase)? Domain [0,2], period 2/m? Use 2π*m? We need zero mean; sin(π*m*x) on [0,2] has zero mean for integer m. Use freq m with np.sin(np.pi*m*x+phase) not zero mean if phase. Better zero f mean after.
  f -= f.mean()
  return f
```

def make_candidate(best,n,rng,idx):
  if idx%5==0:
    f=rng.normal(size=n); f -= f.mean(); f = np.convolve(f, np.ones(5)/5, mode='same'); f-=f.mean(); h=best+0.05*f
  elif ... sine with freqs [1,2,3]
  elif binary blocks: h = (rng.uniform(0,1,n) < 0.5).astype(float); moving average 5; project
  elif sym: x=... reverse; h=0.5*(best+1-reverse(best))
  else blend: h=0.8*best+0.2*binary + noise
  return project_feasible(h,n)

`coord_step` details:
```
def coord_step(c, h, p, n, nbisect=30):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14: return h0
    lo = max(0, n-1-p); hi = min(2*n-2, n-1+p)
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
    tau = ms - (n-1)
    jf = p + tau; jb = p - tau
    vf = (jf >=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    diag = ms == n-1
    has_diag = bool(np.any(diag))
    if has_diag:
       di = int(np.flatnonzero(diag)[0]); c_diag=float(c_aff[di]); a[di]=0.0
    else: c_diag=0.0
    non = ~diag if has_diag else np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
    if has_diag:
       idx_nd = ms[non]
       c_nd = c_aff[non]; a_nd = a[non]
    else:
       idx_nd = ms; c_nd = c_aff; a_nd = a
    zero_max=-1.0; eps=1e-13
    pos_idx = np.flatnonzero(a_nd > eps)
    neg_idx = np.flatnonzero(a_nd < -eps)
    pos_a = a_nd[pos_idx]; pos_c = c_nd[pos_idx]
    neg_a = a_nd[neg_idx]; neg_c = c_nd[neg_idx]
    if pos_idx.size+neg_idx.size < c_nd.size:
       zero_max = float(np.max(c_nd[np.abs(a_nd) <= eps])) if c_nd.size else -1.0
    unaff=0.0
    if lo>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[:lo])))
    if hi<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[hi+1:])))
    cur=float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= cur - 1e-12: return h0
    base_diag = h0*(1-h0)
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=cur
    do_diag = has_diag
    for _ in range(nbisect):
       if Lhi-Llo < 1e-14: break
       T=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       ok,_,_ = coord_feasible(T, unaff, pos_a,pos_c,neg_a,neg_c,zero_max,h0,c_diag,base_diag,do_diag)
       if ok: Lhi=T
       else: Llo=T
    T=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
    ok,xlo,xhi = coord_feasible(T, unaff, pos_a,pos_c,neg_a,neg_c,zero_max,h0,c_diag,base_diag,do_diag)
    if not ok:
       return h0
    x = float(np.clip(0.5*(xlo+xhi),0,1))
    if abs(x-h0) < 1e-14: return h0
    # update c
    if has_diag:
       c[idx_nd] += a_nd*(x-h0)
       c[ms[di]] += x*(1-x) - base_diag
    else:
       c[ms] += a*(x-h0)
    return x
```

def try_pair_inplace(h,p,q,n,grid,current_count):
   hp=float(h[p]); hq=float(h[q]); s=hp+hq
   tlo=max(0.0,s-1.0); thi=min(1.0,s)
   if thi-tlo < 1e-11: return False,current_count
   ts=np.linspace(tlo,thi,grid)
   # add specific
   pts=[hp, 0.5*(tlo+thi)] + [hp+d, hp-d for d in (0.001,0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1)]
   ts=np.concatenate((ts,np.array(pts)))
   ts=ts[(ts>=tlo-1e-12)&(ts<=thi+1e-12)]
   ts=np.clip(ts,tlo,thi)
   # unique with tolerance? np.unique enough maybe
   ts=np.unique(ts)
   best_t=hp; best_u=hq; best_count=current_count
   pts_list=ts # not needed
   for tval in ts:
      t=float(tval); u=s-t
      if u< -1e-12 or u>1+1e-12: continue
      if u<0: u=0.0; t=s-u
      elif u>1: u=1.0; t=s-u
      h[p]=t; h[q]=u
      cnt=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
      if cnt < best_count - 1e-15:
          best_count=cnt; best_t=t; best_u=u
   # local refine using best_t
   if best_count < current_count - 1e-14:
      span=(thi-tlo)
      if best_t <= ts[0]+1e-12: lo=tlo; hi=min(thi,best_t+span/grid)
      elif best_t >= ts[-1]-1e-12: hi=thi; lo=max(tlo,best_t-span/grid)
      else:
         lo=max(tlo,best_t-span/grid); hi=min(thi,best_t+span/grid)
      ts2=np.linspace(lo,hi,17)
      for tval in ts2: ...
      h[p]=best_t; h[q]=best_u
      final_cnt=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
      return True, final_cnt
   h[p]=hp; h[q]=hq
   return False, current_count
```

`active_pair_list`: Need if rng is None? We'll pass rng always. For deterministic tie if no rng? Use rng.
```
def active_pair_list(h,n,c,rng,top_m=4,top_terms=8,rand_pairs=22):
  k=min(top_m,len(c)); idx=np.argpartition(c, len(c)-k)[-k:] if len(c)>1 else np.array([0])
  pairs=[]; seen=set()
  for m_val in idx:
    mi=int(m_val); tau=mi-(n-1)
    if tau>=0:
      js=np.arange(tau,n); ts=js-tau
    else:
      js=np.arange(0,n+tau); ts=js-tau
    if js.size==0: continue
    vals=h[js]*(1-h[ts])
    tt=min(top_terms, js.size)
    if js.size>tt:
       top=np.argpartition(vals, js.size-tt)[-tt:]
    else: top=np.arange(js.size)
    for ii in top:
       a=int(js[ii]); b=int(ts[ii])
       if a==b:
          q=int(rng.randint(n))
           if rng is not None else (a+1)%n
          b=q
       if a==b: continue
       if a>b: a,b=b,a
       key=(a,b)
       if key not in seen: seen.add(key); pairs.append(key)
  for _ in range(rand_pairs):
     a=int(rng.randint(n)); b=int(rng.randint(n))
     if a>b: a,b=b,a
     if a==b: continue
     key=(a,b)
     if key not in seen: seen.add(key); pairs.append(key)
  return pairs
```
If tau=0, js=ts all same, top terms diagonal. We pair with random q. Good.

Could pair list be huge? top_m*top_terms+rand ~54. OK.

`try_pairs` signature:
```
def try_pairs(h,n,deadline,grid=33,max_sweeps=None,top_m=4,top_terms=8,rand_pairs=22,rng=None):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
   count=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')))
   sweeps=0
   while time.time() < deadline and (max_sweeps is None or sweeps < max_sweeps):
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
      curc=float(np.max(c))
      if curc < count: count=curc
      pairs=active_pair_list(h,n,c,rng,top_m,top_terms,rand_pairs)
      improved=False
      for p,q in pairs:
         if time.time() >= deadline: break
         ok,cnt=try_pair_inplace(h,p,q,n,grid,count)
         if ok:
            count=cnt; improved=True
      sweeps +=1
      if not improved: break
   return h,count
```
Important: The `count` variable is best so far. Within sweep, if a pair improves from previous candidate, we set. But `try_pair_inplace` receives current h and current_count. If no improve, count unchanged. Good.
However, if after many pairs, c array from sweep start stale. That's fine. At next sweep recompute. If count improved, active pairs in same sweep may be less optimal but ok.
If no pair improves in a sweep, break. Could there be improvement after multiple pairs that individually not improve from current but together? The search is greedy; may miss. Coordinate can help. Could run a second random sweep if no improvement? Break to save time.

In `try_pair_inplace`, if current_count already lower than actual max due to prior counts? Input h has max current_count from last improve. If not, it may miss. We call after enforce? If enforce changed, at start of try_pairs recompute count. Within, count is exact after last pair (we recompute final_cnt). Good.

Now `local_optimize`:
```
def local_optimize(h,n,deadline,rng,use_pairs_first=True):
  h=enforce_sum(h,n)
  c=compute_c5(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
  # pair phase split 45%
  remaining=deadline-time.time()
  if use_pairs_first and remaining>10:
     pair_end=min(deadline, time.time()+0.45*remaining)
     h2,c2=try_pairs(h,n,pair_end,grid=33,max_sweeps=None,top_m=4,top_terms=8,rand_pairs=22,rng=rng)
     h2=enforce_sum(h2,n); c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
     if c2<best_c: best_h,best_c=h2,c2
  # coordinate phase
  if time.time()+5 < deadline:
     h3=best_h.copy()
     h3,c3=coord_descent(h3,n,80,deadline,rng,True,30) # deadline absolute
     if c3<best_c: best_h,best_c=h3,c3
  # final pair polish remaining
  if time.time()+10 < deadline:
     h4=best_h.copy()
     h4,c4=try_pairs(h4,n,deadline-2,grid=41,max_sweeps=None,top_m=6,top_terms=10,rand_pairs=30,rng=rng)
     h4=enforce_sum(h4,n); c4=compute_c5(h4,n)
     if c4<best_c: best_h,best_c=h4,c4
  return best_h,best_c
```
But `coord_descent` has deadline absolute; if pair_end passed, deadline still original. Good. However local_optimize used in trials with a slice; if deadline is final, it will use all remaining, not slice. For trials we need pass a slice deadline (absolute) that is before final. ```
def resample_to(h,n_target):
  if len(h)==n_target: return h.copy()
  x0=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n_target); return np.interp(x1,x0,h)
```

`make_candidate(best,n,rng,typ)`: best already length n? Pass resampled.
```
def make_candidate(base,n,rng,typ):
  h=base.copy()
  r=float(rng.rand())
  if typ==0:
     f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); f=np.convolve(f,np.ones(5)/5,'same'); f-=f.mean(); h += 0.05*f
  elif typ==1:
     x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
     f=np.zeros(n)
     for m in (1,2,3,5):
        f += rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1)*np.sin(np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
     f-=f.mean(); h += 0.1*f
  elif typ==2:
     b=(rng.rand(n)<0.5).astype(float); b=np.convolve(b,np.ones(7)/7,'same'); h=0.7*base+0.3*b
  elif typ==3:
     rev=base[::-1].copy(); h=0.5*(base + 1.0 - rev)
  else:
     f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); h += 0.15*f - 0.15*f.mean()
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```
The up to 32 scalar literal rule: `(1,2,3,5)` 4 ok. `np.ones(5)` length 5 created algorithmically. Good.
Maybe use `base[::-1]` not interp; since C invariant under reversal, sym. OK.

Detailed code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def corr_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def max_count(h):
    return float(np.max(corr_counts(h)))

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return max_count(h) * (2.0 / float(n))

def project_feasible(y, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.size == 1:
       h = np.array([target], dtype=float)
       return np.clip(h,0,1)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    if lo > hi: lo,hi=hi,lo
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y+mid,0.0,1.0)))
       if s > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    h=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff)<1e-11: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       if not np.any(mask): break
       h[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>1e-9:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)) if diff>0 else int(np.argmin(h))
       h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0.0,1.0))
       # if still, scale?
    return h
```

def top_shifts(c,k):
   k=int(min(k,len(c)))
   if k<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
   if len(c)==1: return np.array([0],dtype=int)
   return np.argpartition(c, len(c)-k)[-k:].astype(int)

def try_pair_inplace(h,p,q,n,grid,current_count):
    hp=float(h[p]); hq=float(h[q]); s=hp+hq

    first_best=True

       if u < -1e-12 or u > 1.0+1e-12: continue

Now `coord_feasible` code. Let's write carefully.
```
def coord_feasible(T, unaff, pos_a,pos_c,neg_a,neg_c,zero_max,h0,c_diag,base_diag,has_diag):
    if T < unaff - 1e-13: return (False,0.0,0.0)
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0; eps=1e-13
    if pos_a.size:
       b=np.min(h0 + (T - pos_c) / pos_a)
       if b < xhi: xhi=float(b)
    if neg_a.size:
       b=np.max(h0 + (T - neg_c) / neg_a)
       if b > xlo: xlo=float(b)
    if xlo < 0.0: xlo=0.0
    if xhi > 1.0: xhi=1.0
    if zero_max > T + eps: return (False,0.0,0.0)
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12: return (False,0.0,0.0)
    if has_diag:
       D = T - c_diag + base_diag
       if D < -1e-13: return (False,0.0,0.0)
       if D < 0.25 - 1e-13:
           disc=1.0-4.0*D
           if disc<0: disc=0.0
           rt=float(np.sqrt(disc)); r1=0.5*(1-rt); r2=0.5*(1+rt)
           if not (xhi <= r1 + 1e-13 or xlo >= r2 - 1e-13):
               left = min(xhi, r1) - xlo
               right = xhi - max(xlo, r2)
               if left <= -1e-12 and right <= -1e-12:
                   return (False,0.0,0.0)
               if xlo < r1 and xhi > r2:
                   if h0 <= r1 and left > -1e-10:
                       xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                   elif h0 >= r2 and right > -1e-10:
                       xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                   elif left >= right:
                       xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                   else:
                       xlo = max(xlo, r2)
               elif xlo < r1:
                   xhi = min(xhi, r1)
               else:
                   xlo = max(xlo, r2)
               if xlo < 0: xlo=0
               if xhi > 1: xhi=1
               if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
                   # fallback: try other side
                   if left > -1e-10:
                       xhi=min(xhi,r1); xlo=min(xlo,r1)
                       if xlo<0: xlo=0
                   elif right > -1e-10:
                       xlo=max(xlo,r2); xhi=max(xhi,r2)
                       if xhi>1: xhi=1
                   else:
                       return (False,0.0,0.0)
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12: return (False,0.0,0.0)
    return (True,xlo,xhi)
```
Fallback may create invalid: If we set `xlo=min(xlo,r1)` and xlo originally >r1? But condition left > -1e-10 implies min(xhi,r1)-xlo >= -eps, so xlo <= r1 roughly. OK. For right similar.

One subtlety: For line constraints with `a` negative, formula `h0 + (T-c)/a`. Since a negative, if T-c positive, bound negative; xlo may be set to smaller? It uses max. OK.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-5.0
    if deadline <= t0 + 10: deadline=t0+max(10,float(budget_s)-1)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_init=int(len(h_init))
    except Exception:
       n_init=700
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n_init)
       h_init=0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.1*np.sin(3*np.pi*x)
       h_init=np.clip(h_init,0,1)
    if n_init < 20: ...? maybe set 700? If initial too small, can still. But target.
    h_init=project_feasible(h_init,n_init)
    best_h=h_init.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h_init,n_init); best_n=n_init
    # Parent polish
    parent_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(20.0, 0.45*float(budget_s)))
    h2,c2=local_optimize(best_h,best_n,parent_end,rng)
    if c2 < best_c:
       best_h,best_c,best_n=h2,c2,h2.size
    # trials
    n_cands=[]
    # add parent and variants
    for nc in (best_n, max(60,best_n//3), max(90,best_n//2), int(best_n*0.75), int(best_n*1.2), int(best_n*1.5)):
        nc=int(nc)
        if 40 <= nc <= 1000:
           if nc not in n_cands: n_cands.append(nc)
    # Sort? maybe near parent first? Use order as listed.
    trials_done=0
    max_trials=6
    for nc in n_cands:
        if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
        for trial_idx in range(3):
            trials_done += 1
            if trials_done > max_trials or time.time() > deadline - 120: break
            remaining = deadline - 120 - time.time()
            if remaining < 30: break
            alloc = max(20.0, min(90.0, remaining / float(max(1, max_trials - trials_done + 1))))
            trial_deadline = min(deadline - 120, time.time() + alloc)
            if trial_deadline <= time.time() + 5: break
            base=resample_to(best_h,nc)
            cand=make_candidate(base,nc,rng,trial_idx)
            h2,c2=local_optimize(cand,nc,trial_deadline,rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h,best_c,best_n=h2,c2,h2.size
    # Final polish all remaining
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        h2,c2=local_optimize(best_h,best_n,deadline,rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=h2,c2
    best_h=enforce_sum(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def local_optimize(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h=enforce_sum(h,n); c=compute_c5
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    remaining=deadline-time.time()
    if remaining > 20:
       pair_end=min(deadline, time.time()+0.4*remaining)
       h2,c2=try_pairs(h,n,pair_end,grid=33,max_sweeps=None,top_m=4,top_terms=8,rand_pairs=22,rng=rng)
       h2=enforce_sum(h2,n); c2=compute_c5
       if c2<best_c: best_h,best_c=h2,c2
    if time.time()+5 < deadline:
       h3=best_h.copy()
       h3,c3=coord_descent(h3,n,100,deadline,rng,True,30)
       if c3<best_c: best_h,best_c=h3,c3
    if time.time()+10 < deadline:
       h4=best_h.copy()
       h4,c4=try_pairs(h4,n,deadline-2,grid=41,max_sweeps=None,top_m=6,top_terms=10,rand_pairs=30,rng=rng)
       h4=enforce_sum(h4,n); c4=compute_c5
       if c4<best_c: best_h,best_c=h4,c4
    return best_h,best_c
```

Now, check exact constraints: `sum(h)*dx =1` equivalently sum(h)==n/2 exactly. Our h floats maybe sum close not exact. We can at final force one coordinate. Implement `make_sum_exact` at end. Better than `enforce_sum` approx.
```
def make_sum_exact(h,n):
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # adjust one coordinate repeatedly
    for _ in range(10):
       s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(h)); add=max(diff,-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
    if abs(float(np.sum(h))-target)>1e-12:
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return h
```

```
def pair_eval(h,p,q,s,t):
    u=s-t
    if u < 0.0:
       u=0.0; t=s-u
    elif u > 1.0:
       u=1.0; t=s-u
    t=float(np.clip(t,0.0,1.0))
    u=float(s-t)
    if u < 0.0: u=0.0; t=s
    if u > 1.0: u=1.0; t=s-u
    h[p]=t; h[q]=u
    cnt=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
    return t,u,cnt
```

    h=enforce_sum(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)

       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       improved=False

```
def make_candidate(base,n,rng,typ):
  h=base.copy()
  if typ==0:
    f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); h += 0.05*f
  elif typ==1:
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n); f=(np.sin(np.pi*x+rng.uniform(0,6.28))+0.5*np.sin(3*np.pi*x+rng.uniform(0,6.28))); f-=f.mean(); h += 0.08*f
  elif typ==2:
    b=(rng.rand(n)<0.5).astype(float); h=0.85*base+0.15*b
  else:
    h=0.5*(base + 1.0 - base[::-1]); f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); h += 0.03*f
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def maxc(h):
    return float(np.max(corr(h)))

def c5(h,n):
    return maxc(h)*2.0/float(n)

def project(y,n):
    t=0.5*float(n)
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    if n<=1:
        return np.array([min(1.0,max(0.0,t))])
    lo=float(np.min(y))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(y))+1.0
    if lo>hi: lo,hi=hi,lo
    for _ in range(50):
        m=0.5*(lo+hi); s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y+m,0.0,1.0)))
        if s>t: hi=m
        else: lo=m
    h=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(15):
        d=t-float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d)<1e-11: break
        msk=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
        if not np.any(msk): break
        h[msk]+=d/float(np.sum(msk)); np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    d=t-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(d)>1e-9:
        i=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)) if d>0 else int(np.argmin(h))
        h[i]=float(np.clip(h[i]+d,0.0,1.0))
    return h

def exact_sum(h,n):
    h=h.copy(); h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        d=0.5*float(n)-float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d)<1e-14: break
        if d>0:
            i=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); h[i]=min(1.0,h[i]+d)
        else:
            i=int(np.argmin(h)); h[i]=max(0.0,h[i]+d)
    if abs(0.5*float(n)-float(np.sum(h)))>1e-10:
        h=project(h,n)
    return h

def top_shifts(a,k):
    k=int(min(k,len(a)))
    if k<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    if len(a)==1: return np.array([0],dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(a,len(a)-k)[-k:].astype(int)

def active_pairs(h,n,c,rng):
    idx=top_shifts(c,4)
    out=[]; seen=set()
    for mi in idx:
        tau=int(mi-(n-1))
        if tau>=0:
            js=np.arange(tau,n); ts=js-tau
        else:
            js=np.arange(0,n+tau); ts=js-tau
        if js.size==0: continue
        vals=h[js]*(1.0-h[ts])
        k=min(8,js.size)
        if js.size>k:
            top=np.argpartition(vals,js.size-k)[-k:]
        else:
            top=np.arange(js.size)
        for ii in top:
            a=int(js[ii]); b=int(ts[ii])
            if a==b:
                b=int(rng.randint(n))
            if a==b: continue
            if a>b: a,b=b,a
            if (a,b) not in seen:
                seen.add((a,b)); out.append((a,b))
    for _ in range(24):
        a=int(rng.randint(n)); b=int(rng.randint(n))
        if a==b: continue
        if a>b: a,b=b,a
        if (a,b) not in seen:
            seen.add((a,b)); out.append((a,b))
    return out

def pair_eval(h,p,q,s,t):
    u=s-t
    if u<0.0: u=0.0; t=s-u
    elif u>1.0: u=1.0; t=s-u
    t=float(np.clip(t,0.0,1.0)); u=float(s-t)
    if u<0.0: u=0.0; t=s
    if u>1.0: u=1.0; t=s-u
    h[p]=t; h[q]=u
    return t,u,float(np.max(corr(h)))

def try_pair(h,p,q,n,grid,current):
    hp=float(h[p]); hq=float(h[q]); s=hp+hq
    lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s)
    if hi-lo<1e-11: return False,current
    g=int(grid)
    ts=np.linspace(lo,hi,g)
    ex=[hp,0.5*(lo+hi)]
    for d in (0.001,0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1):
        ex.append(hp+d); ex.append(hp-d)
    ts=np.concatenate((ts,np.array(ex,float)))
    ts=ts[(ts>=lo-1e-12)&(ts<=hi+1e-12)]
    ts=np.unique(np.clip(ts,lo,hi))
    best_t,best_u,best=hp,hq,current
    for tv in ts:
        t,u,v=pair_eval(h,p,q,s,float(tv))
        if v<best-1e-15: best,best_t,best_u=v,t,u
    if best<current-1e-14:
        span=hi-lo
        if len(ts)>1:
            if best_t<=ts[0]+1e-12: rlo, rhi=lo,min(hi,best_t+span/g)
            elif best_t>=ts[-1]-1e-12: rlo,rhi=max(lo,best_t-span/g),hi
            else: rlo,rhi=max(lo,best_t-span/g),min(hi,best_t+span/g)
        else: rlo,rhi=lo,hi
        if rhi<rlo: rlo,rhi=rhi,rlo
        for tv in np.linspace(rlo,rhi,17):
            t,u,v=pair_eval(h,p,q,s,float(tv))
            if v<best-1e-15: best,best_t,best_u=v,t,u
        h[p]=best_t; h[q]=best_u
        return True,float(np.max(corr(h)))
    h[p]=hp; h[q]=hq
    return False,current

def try_pairs(h,n,deadline,rng,grid=33,top_m=4,top_terms=8):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(1234)
    best=float(np.max(corr(h)))
    sweeps=0
    while time.time()<deadline and sweeps<200:
        c=corr(h)
        cur=float(np.max(c))
        if cur<best: best=cur
        pairs=active_pairs(h,n,c,rng)
        improved=False
        for p,q in pairs:
            if time.time()>=deadline: break
            ok,val=try_pair(h,p,q,n,grid,best)
            if ok:
                best=val; improved=True
        sweeps+=1
        if not improved: break
    return h,best
```
Note active_pairs currently ignores top_m/top_terms params. We can remove from try_pairs signature or set fixed. In local final, want more top_m/terms? We can modify active_pairs to accept optional but default fixed; try_pairs pass. Code length. Use fixed 4/8/24; for final maybe okay. Could add params but extra lines. Keep fixed. If want final stronger, not necessary.

`coord_feasible` compressed:
```
def coord_feas(T,unaff,pa,pc,na,nc,zmax,h0,cdi,base,has):
    if T<unaff-1e-13: return False,0.0,0.0
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    if pa.size:
        b=float(np.min(h0+(T-pc)/pa))
        if b<xhi: xhi=b
    if na.size:
        b=float(np.max(h0+(T-nc)/na))
        if b>xlo: xlo=b
    if xlo<0: xlo=0.0
    if xhi>1: xhi=1.0
    if zmax>T+1e-13: return False,0.0,0.0
    if xlo>xhi+1e-12: return False,0.0,0.0
    if has:
        D=T-cdi+base
        if D<-1e-13: return False,0.0,0.0
        if D<0.25-1e-13:
            disc=1.0-4.0*D
            if disc<0: disc=0.0
            rt=float(np.sqrt(disc)); r1=0.5*(1.0-rt); r2=0.5*(1.0+rt)
            if not (xhi<=r1+1e-13 or xlo>=r2-1e-13):
                left=min(xhi,r1)-xlo; right=xhi-max(xlo,r2)
                if left<=-1e-12 and right<=-1e-12: return False,0.0,0.0
                if xlo<r1 and xhi>r2:
                    if h0<=r1 and left>-1e-10: xhi=min(xhi,r1)
                    elif h0>=r2 and right>-1e-10: xlo=max(xlo,r2)
                    elif left>=right: xhi=min(xhi,r1)
                    else: xlo=max(xlo,r2)
                elif xlo<r1: xhi=min(xhi,r1)
                else: xlo=max(xlo,r2)
                if xlo<0: xlo=0.0
                if xhi>1: xhi=1.0
                if xlo>xhi+1e-12:
                    if left>-1e-10:
                        xhi=min(xhi,r1); xlo=min(xlo,r1)
                    elif right>-1e-10:
                        xlo=max(xlo,r2); xhi=max(xhi,r2)
                    else: return False,0.0,0.0
    if xlo>xhi+1e-12: return False,0.0,0.0
    return True,xlo,xhi
```
OK.

`coord_step` compressed:
```
def coord_step(c,h,p,n,nb):
    h0=float(h[p])
    if h0<=1e-14 or h0>=1.0-1e-14: return h0
    lo=max(0,n-1-p); hi=min(2*n-2,n-1+p)
    ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1); tau=ms-(n-1)
    jf=p+tau; jb=p-tau
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf): a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    c_aff=c[ms]; diag=ms==n-1
    has=False; di=0; cdi=0.0
    if np.any(diag):
        has=True; di=int(np.flatnonzero(diag)[0]); cdi=float(c_aff[di]); a[di]=0.0
        non=~diag; idx_nd=ms[non]; cnds=c_aff[non]; a_nd=a[non]
    else:
        non=np.ones(len(ms),bool); idx_nd=ms; cnds=c_aff; a_nd=a
    eps=1e-13; zmax=-1.0
    pos=np.flatnonzero(a_nd>eps); neg=np.flatnonzero(a_nd<-eps)
    if cnds.size:
       if np.any(np.abs(a_nd)<=eps): zmax=float(np.max(cnds[np.abs(a_nd)<=eps]))
    pa=a_nd[pos]; pc=cnds[pos]; na=a_nd[neg]; nc=cnds[neg]
    unaff=0.0
    if lo>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[:lo])))
    if hi<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[hi+1:])))
    cur=float(np.max(c))
    if unaff>=cur-1e-12: return h0
    base_diag=h0*(1.0-h0)
    Llo=float(unaff); Lhi=float(cur)
    for _ in range(nb):
        if Lhi-Llo<1e-14: break
        T=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
        ok,_,_=coord_feas(T,unaff,pa,pc,na,nc,zmax,h0,cdi,base_diag,has)
        if ok: Lhi=T
        else: Llo=T
    T=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
    ok,xlo,xhi=coord_feas(T,unaff,pa,pc,na,nc,zmax,h0,cdi,base_diag,has)
    if not ok: return h0
    x=float(np.clip(0.5*(xlo+xhi),0.0,1.0))
    if abs(x-h0)<1e-14: return h0
    if has:
        c[idx_nd]+=a_nd*(x-h0)
        c[ms[di]]=cdi+x*(1.0-x)-base_diag
    else:
        c[ms]+=a*(x-h0)
    return x
```
Need `non=np.ones(len(ms),bool)` syntax? `np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)` better. Use `bool` may fail? np.ones(5,bool) works but bool is Python type alias for np.bool? OK. Use `dtype=bool`.

`coord_descent`:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,random=True,nb=32):
    h=exact_sum(h,n)
    best=h.copy(); bestd=c5(h,n)
    c=corr(h)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        imp=False
        order=rng.permutation(n) if (random and rng is not None) else np.arange(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time()>=deadline: break
            h0=float(h[p])
            if h0<=1e-14 or h0>=1.0-1e-14: continue
            x=coord_step(c,h,p,n,nb)
            if abs(x-h0)>1e-13:
                h[p]=x; imp=True
        if not imp: break
        h=project(h,n)
        val=c5(h,n)
        if val<bestd: best,h2=h.copy(),val
        elif val>bestd+1e-10:
            h=best.copy()
        c=corr(h)
    h=best.copy(); h=exact_sum(h,n); val=c5(h,n)
    return h,(val if val<bestd else bestd)
```
Need assign correctly:

if val < bestd:
    best=h.copy(); bestd=val
else:
    h=best? Wait if not improved, we still keep projected h? Maybe restore best if worse.
```

`local_optimize`:
```
def local_opt(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=exact_sum(h,n)
    best=h.copy(); bestd=c5(h,n)
    remaining=deadline-time.time()
    if remaining>15:
        pend=min(deadline,time.time()+0.4*remaining)
        h2,v2=try_pairs(h,n,pend,rng,grid=33)
        h2=exact_sum(h2,n); v2=c5(h2,n)
        if v2<bestd: best,bestd=h2,v2
    if time.time()+5<deadline:
        h3=best.copy()
        h3,v3=coord_descent(h3,n,200,deadline,rng,True,32)
        if v3<bestd: best,bestd=h3,v3
    if time.time()+10<deadline:
        h4=best.copy()
        h4,v4=try_pairs(h4,n,deadline-2,rng,grid=49)
        h4=exact_sum(h4,n); v4=c5(h4,n)
        if v4<bestd: best,bestd=h4,v4
    h=best.copy(); h=exact_sum(h,n)
    return h,c5(h,n)
```
Good.

`make_candidate`:
```
def make_candidate(base,n,rng,typ):
    h=base.copy()
    if typ==0:
        f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); h+=0.05*f
    elif typ==1:
        x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
        f=np.sin(np.pi*x+rng.uniform(0,6.28))+0.5*np.sin(3*np.pi*x+rng.uniform(0,6.28))
        f-=f.mean(); h+=0.08*f
    elif typ==2:
        b=(rng.rand(n)<0.5).astype(float); h=0.85*base+0.15*b
    else:
        hh=0.5*(base+1.0-base[::-1]); f=rng.normal(size=n); f-=f.mean(); h=0.9*hh+0.03*f
    return project(h,n)
```

`run`:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-5.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
       n0=700; x=np.linspace(0,2,n0)
       h0=0.5+0.25*np.sin(np.pi*x)+0.1*np.sin(3*np.pi*x); h0=np.clip(h0,0,1)
    h0=exact_sum(h0,n0)
    best=h0.copy(); bestd=c5(h0,n0)
    parent_end=min(deadline,t0+max(25.0,0.45*float(budget_s)))
    h1,v1=local_opt(best,n0,parent_end,rng)
    if v1<bestd: best,bestd=h1,v1
    if time.time()<deadline-90:
        for typ in (0,1,2,3):
            if time.time()>deadline-90: break
            rem=deadline-90-time.time()
            if rem<25: break
            alloc=min(80.0, max(20.0, rem/4.0))
            td=min(deadline-90,time.time()+alloc)
            cand=make_candidate(best,n0,rng,typ)
            h2,v2=local_opt(cand,n0,td,rng)
            if v2<bestd: best,bestd=h2,v2
    if time.time()<deadline-3:
        h3,v3=local_opt(best,n0,deadline,rng)
        if v3<bestd: best,bestd=h3,v3
    best=exact_sum(best,n0)
    bestd=c5(best,n0)
    return best,bestd,n0
```

import numpy as np
import time

def corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def maxc(h):
    return float(np.max(corr(h)))

def c5(h, n):
    return maxc(h) * 2.0 / float(n)

def project(y, n):
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([min(1.0, max(0.0, t))])
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    if lo > hi:
        lo, hi = hi, lo
    for _ in range(50):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y + m, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > t:
            hi = m
        else:
            lo = m
    h = np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(15):
        d = t - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d) < 1e-11:
            break
        msk = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if not np.any(msk):
            break
        h[msk] += d / float(np.sum(msk))
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    d = t - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(d) > 1e-9:
        i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmin(h))
        h[i] = float(np.clip(h[i] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h

def fix_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(8):
        d = t - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + d)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmin(h))
            h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + d)
    if abs(0.5 * float(n) - float(np.sum(h))) > 1e-10:
        h = project(h, n)
    return h

def top_shifts(a, k):
    k = int(min(k, len(a)))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if len(a) == 1:
        return np.array([0], dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(a, len(a) - k)[-k:].astype(int)

def active_pairs(h, n, c, rng):
    out = []
    for mi in top_shifts(c, 4):
        tau = int(mi - (n - 1))
        if tau >= 0:
            js = np.arange(tau, n)
            ts = js - tau
        else:
            js = np.arange(0, n + tau)
            ts = js - tau
        if js.size == 0:
            continue
        vals = h[js] * (1.0 - h[ts])
        k = min(8, js.size)
        if js.size > k:
            top = np.argpartition(vals, js.size - k)[-k:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(js.size)
        for ii in top:
            a = int(js[ii])
            b = int(ts[ii])
            if a == b:
                b = int(rng.randint(n))
            if a == b:
                continue
            if a > b:
                a, b = b, a
            out.append((a, b))
    for _ in range(22):
        a = int(rng.randint(n))
        b = int(rng.randint(n))
        if a > b:
            a, b = b, a
        out.append((a, b))
    return out

def pair_eval(h, p, q, s, t):
    u = s - t
    if u < 0.0:
        u = 0.0
        t = s - u
    elif u > 1.0:
        u = 1.0
        t = s - u
    t = float(np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0))
    u = float(s - t)
    if u < 0.0:
        u = 0.0
        t = s
    if u > 1.0:
        u = 1.0
        t = s - u
    h[p] = t
    h[q] = u
    return t, u, float(np.max(corr(h)))

def try_pair(h, p, q, n, grid, current):
    hp = float(h[p])
    hq = float(h[q])
    s = hp + hq
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo < 1e-11:
        return False, current
    g = int(grid)
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, g)
    ex = [hp, 0.5 * (lo + hi)]
    for d in (0.001, 0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
        ex.append(hp + d)
        ex.append(hp - d)
    ts = np.concatenate((ts, np.array(ex, dtype=float)))
    ts = ts[(ts >= lo - 1e-12) & (ts <= hi + 1e-12)]
    ts = np.unique(np.clip(ts, lo, hi))
    best_t, best_u, best = hp, hq, current
    for tv in ts:
        t, u, v = pair_eval(h, p, q, s, float(tv))
        if v < best - 1e-15:
            best, best_t, best_u = v, t, u
    if best < current - 1e-14:
        span = hi - lo
        if len(ts) > 1:
            if best_t <= ts[0] + 1e-12:
                rlo, rhi = lo, min(hi, best_t + span / g)
            elif best_t >= ts[-1] - 1e-12:
                rlo, rhi = max(lo, best_t - span / g), hi
            else:
                rlo, rhi = max(lo, best_t - span / g), min(hi, best_t + span / g)
        else:
            rlo, rhi = lo, hi
        if rhi < rlo:
            rlo, rhi = rhi, rlo
        for tv in np.linspace(rlo, rhi, 17):
            t, u, v = pair_eval(h, p, q, s, float(tv))
            if v < best - 1e-15:
                best, best_t, best_u = v, t, u
        h[p] = best_t
        h[q] = best_u
        return True, float(np.max(corr(h)))
    h[p] = hp
    h[q] = hq
    return False, current

def try_pairs(h, n, deadline, rng, grid=33):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(1234)
    best = float(np.max(corr(h)))
    sweeps = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < 200:
        c = corr(h)
        cur = float(np.max(c))
        if cur < best:
            best = cur
        pairs = active_pairs(h, n, c, rng)
        improved = False
        for p, q in pairs:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            if p == q:
                continue
            ok, val = try_pair(h, p, q, n, grid, best)
            if ok:
                best = val
                improved = True
        sweeps += 1
        if not improved:
            break
    return h, best

def coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base, has):
    if T < unaff - 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    xlo = 0.0
    xhi = 1.0
    if pa.size:
        b = float(np.min(h0 + (T - pc) / pa))
        if b < xhi:
            xhi = b
    if na.size:
        b = float(np.max(h0 + (T - ac) / na))
        if b > xlo:
            xlo = b
    if xlo < 0.0:
        xlo = 0.0
    if xhi > 1.0:
        xhi = 1.0
    if zmax > T + 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if has:
        D = T - cdi + base
        if D < -1e-13:
            return False, 0.0, 0.0
        if D < 0.25 - 1e-13:
            disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * D
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0
            rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
            r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
            r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
            if not (xhi <= r1 + 1e-13 or xlo >= r2 - 1e-13):
                left = min(xhi, r1) - xlo
                right = xhi - max(xlo, r2)
                if left <= -1e-12 and right <= -1e-12:
                    return False, 0.0, 0.0
                if xlo < r1 and xhi > r2:
                    if h0 <= r1 and left > -1e-10:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                    elif h0 >= r2 and right > -1e-10:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                    elif left >= right:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                    else:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                elif xlo < r1:
                    xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                else:
                    xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                if xlo < 0.0:
                    xlo = 0.0
                if xhi > 1.0:
                    xhi = 1.0
                if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
                    if left > -1e-10:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                        xlo = min(xlo, r1)
                    elif right > -1e-10:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                        xhi = max(xhi, r2)
                    else:
                        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    return True, xlo, xhi

def coord_step(c, h, p, n, nb):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
        return h0
    lo = max(0, n - 1 - p)
    hi = min(2 * n - 2, n - 1 + p)
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    tau = ms - (n - 1)
    jf = p + tau
    jb = p - tau
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf):
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb):
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    diag = ms == n - 1
    has = False
    di = 0
    cdi = 0.0
    if np.any(diag):
        has = True
        di = int(np.flatnonzero(diag)[0])
        cdi = float(c_aff[di])
        a[di] = 0.0
        non = ~diag
        idx_nd = ms[non]
        cns = c_aff[non]
        ans = a[non]
    else:
        non = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
        idx_nd = ms
        cns = c_aff
        ans = a
    eps = 1e-13
    zmax = -1.0
    pos = np.flatnonzero(ans > eps)
    neg = np.flatnonzero(ans < -eps)
    if cns.size:
        zmask = np.abs(ans) <= eps
        if np.any(zmask):
            zmax = float(np.max(cns[zmask]))
    pa = ans[pos]
    pc = cns[pos]
    na = ans[neg]
    ac = cns[neg]
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:lo])))
    if hi < 2 * n - 2:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[hi + 1:])))
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= cur - 1e-12:
        return h0
    base_diag = h0 * (1.0 - h0)
    Llo = float(unaff)
    Lhi = float(cur)
    for _ in range(nb):
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-14:
            break
        T = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        ok, _, _ = coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base_diag, has)
        if ok:
            Lhi = T
        else:
            Llo = T
    T = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
    ok, xlo, xhi = coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base_diag, has)
    if not ok:
        return h0
    x = float(np.clip(0.5 * (xlo + xhi), 0.0, 1.0))
    if abs(x - h0) < 1e-14:
        return h0
    if has:
        c[idx_nd] += ans * (x - h0)
        c[ms[di]] = cdi + x * (1.0 - x) - base_diag
    else:
        c[ms] += a * (x - h0)
    return x

def coord_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, random=True, nb=32):
    h = project(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestd = c5(h, n)
    c = corr(h)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        if random and rng is not None:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            order = np.arange(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
                continue
            x = coord_step(c, h, p, n, nb)
            if abs(x - h0) > 1e-13:
                h[p] = x
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        h = project(h, n)
        val = c5(h, n)
        if val < bestd:
            best = h.copy()
            bestd = val
        else:
            h = best.copy()
        c = corr(h)
    h = best.copy()
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    val = c5(h, n)
    return h, min(bestd, val)

def local_opt(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestd = c5(h, n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 15:
        pend = min(deadline, time.time() + 0.4 * rem)
        h2, v2 = try_pairs(h, n, pend, rng, grid=33)
        h2 = project(h2, n)
        v2 = c5(h2, n)
        if v2 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h2, v2
    if time.time() + 5 < deadline:
        h3 = best.copy()
        h3, v3 = coord_descent(h3, n, 220, deadline, rng, True, 32)
        if v3 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h3, v3
    if time.time() + 10 < deadline:
        h4 = best.copy()
        h4, v4 = try_pairs(h4, n, deadline - 2, rng, grid=49)
        h4 = project(h4, n)
        v4 = c5(h4, n)
        if v4 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h4, v4
    h = best.copy()
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    v = c5(h, n)
    return h, min(bestd, v)

def make_candidate(base, n, rng, typ):
    h = base.copy()
    if typ == 0:
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
        f -= f.mean()
        h += 0.05 * f
    elif typ == 1:
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n)
        f = np.sin(np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
        f += 0.5 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
        f -= f.mean()
        h += 0.08 * f
    elif typ == 2:
        b = (rng.rand(n) < 0.5).astype(float)
        h = 0.85 * base + 0.15 * b
    else:
        hh = 0.5 * (base + 1.0 - base[::-1])
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
        f -= f.mean()
        h = 0.9 * hh + 0.03 * f
    return project(h, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 2.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.1 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        h0 = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n0 < 30:
        n0 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.1 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        h0 = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    h0 = project(h0, n0)
    best = h0.copy()
    bestd = c5(h0, n0)

    parent_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(25.0, 0.45 * float(budget_s)))
    h1, v1 = local_opt(best, n0, parent_end, rng)
    if v1 < bestd:
        best, bestd = h1, v1

    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
        for typ in (0, 1, 2, 3):
            if time.time() > deadline - 90:
                break
            rem = deadline - 90 - time.time()
            if rem < 25:
                break
            alloc = min(80.0, max(20.0, rem / 4.0))
            td = min(deadline - 90, time.time() + alloc)
            if td <= time.time() + 4:
                break
            cand = make_candidate(best, n0, rng, typ)
            h2, v2 = local_opt(cand, n0, td, rng)
            if v2 < bestd:
                best, bestd = h2, v2

    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        h3, v3 = local_opt(best, n0, deadline, rng)
        if v3 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h3, v3

    best = fix_sum(best, n0)
    bestd = c5(best, n0)
    return best, float(bestd), int(n0)

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def maxc(h):
    return float(np.max(corr(h)))

def c5(h, n):
    return maxc(h) * 2.0 / float(n)

def project(y, n):
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([min(1.0, max(0.0, t))])
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    if lo > hi:
        lo, hi = hi, lo
    for _ in range(50):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y + m, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > t:
            hi = m
        else:
            lo = m
    h = np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(15):
        d = t - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d) < 1e-11:
            break
        msk = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if not np.any(msk):
            break
        h[msk] += d / float(np.sum(msk))
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    d = t - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(d) > 1e-9:
        i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmin(h))
        h[i] = float(np.clip(h[i] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h

def fix_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(8):
        d = t - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + d)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmin(h))
            h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + d)
    if abs(0.5 * float(n) - float(np.sum(h))) > 1e-10:
        h = project(h, n)
    return h

def top_shifts(a, k):
    k = int(min(k, len(a)))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if len(a) == 1:
        return np.array([0], dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(a, len(a) - k)[-k:].astype(int)

def active_pairs(h, n, c, rng):
    out = []
    for mi in top_shifts(c, 4):
        tau = int(mi - (n - 1))
        if tau >= 0:
            js = np.arange(tau, n)
            ts = js - tau
        else:
            js = np.arange(0, n + tau)
            ts = js - tau
        if js.size == 0:
            continue
        vals = h[js] * (1.0 - h[ts])
        k = min(8, js.size)
        if js.size > k:
            top = np.argpartition(vals, js.size - k)[-k:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(js.size)
        for ii in top:
            a = int(js[ii])
            b = int(ts[ii])
            if a == b:
                b = int(rng.randint(n))
            if a == b:
                continue
            if a > b:
                a, b = b, a
            out.append((a, b))
    for _ in range(22):
        a = int(rng.randint(n))
        b = int(rng.randint(n))
        if a > b:
            a, b = b, a
        out.append((a, b))
    return out

def pair_eval(h, p, q, s, t):
    u = s - t
    if u < 0.0:
        u = 0.0
        t = s - u
    elif u > 1.0:
        u = 1.0
        t = s - u
    t = float(np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0))
    u = float(s - t)
    if u < 0.0:
        u = 0.0
        t = s
    if u > 1.0:
        u = 1.0
        t = s - u
    h[p] = t
    h[q] = u
    return t, u, float(np.max(corr(h)))

def try_pair(h, p, q, n, grid, current):
    hp = float(h[p])
    hq = float(h[q])
    s = hp + hq
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo < 1e-11:
        return False, current
    g = int(grid)
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, g)
    ex = [hp, 0.5 * (lo + hi)]
    for d in (0.001, 0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
        ex.append(hp + d)
        ex.append(hp - d)
    ts = np.concatenate((ts, np.array(ex, dtype=float)))
    ts = ts[(ts >= lo - 1e-12) & (ts <= hi + 1e-12)]
    ts = np.unique(np.clip(ts, lo, hi))
    best_t, best_u, best = hp, hq, current
    for tv in ts:
        t, u, v = pair_eval(h, p, q, s, float(tv))
        if v < best - 1e-15:
            best, best_t, best_u = v, t, u
    if best < current - 1e-14:
        span = hi - lo
        if len(ts) > 1:
            if best_t <= ts[0] + 1e-12:
                rlo, rhi = lo, min(hi, best_t + span / g)
            elif best_t >= ts[-1] - 1e-12:
                rlo, rhi = max(lo, best_t - span / g), hi
            else:
                rlo, rhi = max(lo, best_t - span / g), min(hi, best_t + span / g)
        else:
            rlo, rhi = lo, hi
        if rhi < rlo:
            rlo, rhi = rhi, rlo
        for tv in np.linspace(rlo, rhi, 17):
            t, u, v = pair_eval(h, p, q, s, float(tv))
            if v < best - 1e-15:
                best, best_t, best_u = v, t, u
        h[p] = best_t
        h[q] = best_u
        return True, float(np.max(corr(h)))
    h[p] = hp
    h[q] = hq
    return False, current

def try_pairs(h, n, deadline, rng, grid=33):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(1234)
    best = float(np.max(corr(h)))
    sweeps = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < 200:
        c = corr(h)
        cur = float(np.max(c))
        if cur < best:
            best = cur
        pairs = active_pairs(h, n, c, rng)
        improved = False
        for p, q in pairs:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            if p == q:
                continue
            ok, val = try_pair(h, p, q, n, grid, best)
            if ok:
                best = val
                improved = True
        sweeps += 1
        if not improved:
            break
    return h, best

def coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base, has):
    if T < unaff - 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    xlo = 0.0
    xhi = 1.0
    if pa.size:
        b = float(np.min(h0 + (T - pc) / pa))
        if b < xhi:
            xhi = b
    if na.size:
        b = float(np.max(h0 + (T - ac) / na))
        if b > xlo:
            xlo = b
    if xlo < 0.0:
        xlo = 0.0
    if xhi > 1.0:
        xhi = 1.0
    if zmax > T + 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if has:
        D = T - cdi + base
        if D < -1e-13:
            return False, 0.0, 0.0
        if D < 0.25 - 1e-13:
            disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * D
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0
            rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
            r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
            r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
            if not (xhi <= r1 + 1e-13 or xlo >= r2 - 1e-13):
                left = min(xhi, r1) - xlo
                right = xhi - max(xlo, r2)
                if left <= -1e-12 and right <= -1e-12:
                    return False, 0.0, 0.0
                if xlo < r1 and xhi > r2:
                    if h0 <= r1 and left > -1e-10:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                    elif h0 >= r2 and right > -1e-10:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                    elif left >= right:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                    else:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                elif xlo < r1:
                    xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                else:
                    xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                if xlo < 0.0:
                    xlo = 0.0
                if xhi > 1.0:
                    xhi = 1.0
                if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
                    if left > -1e-10:
                        xhi = min(xhi, r1)
                        xlo = min(xlo, r1)
                    elif right > -1e-10:
                        xlo = max(xlo, r2)
                        xhi = max(xhi, r2)
                    else:
                        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
        return False, 0.0, 0.0
    return True, xlo, xhi

def coord_step(c, h, p, n, nb):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
        return h0
    lo = min(p, n - 1 - p)
    hi = max(2 * n - 2 - p, n - 1 + p)
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    sh = (n - 1) - ms
    jf = p + sh
    jb = p - sh
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf):
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb):
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    diag = ms == n - 1
    has = False
    di = 0
    cdi = 0.0
    if np.any(diag):
        has = True
        di = int(np.flatnonzero(diag)[0])
        cdi = float(c_aff[di])
        a[di] = 0.0
        non = ~diag
        idx_nd = ms[non]
        cns = c_aff[non]
        ans = a[non]
    else:
        non = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
        idx_nd = ms
        cns = c_aff
        ans = a
    eps = 1e-13
    zmax = -1.0
    pos = np.flatnonzero(ans > eps)
    neg = np.flatnonzero(ans < -eps)
    if cns.size:
        zmask = np.abs(ans) <= eps
        if np.any(zmask):
            zmax = float(np.max(cns[zmask]))
    pa = ans[pos]
    pc = cns[pos]
    na = ans[neg]
    ac = cns[neg]
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:lo])))
    if hi < 2 * n - 2:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[hi + 1:])))
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= cur - 1e-12:
        return h0
    base_diag = h0 * (1.0 - h0)
    Llo = float(unaff)
    Lhi = float(cur)
    for _ in range(nb):
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-14:
            break
        T = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        ok, _, _ = coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base_diag, has)
        if ok:
            Lhi = T
        else:
            Llo = T
    T = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
    ok, xlo, xhi = coord_feas(T, unaff, pa, pc, na, ac, zmax, h0, cdi, base_diag, has)
    if not ok:
        return h0
    x = float(np.clip(0.5 * (xlo + xhi), 0.0, 1.0))
    if abs(x - h0) < 1e-14:
        return h0
    if has:
        c[idx_nd] += ans * (x - h0)
        c[ms[di]] = cdi + x * (1.0 - x) - base_diag
    else:
        c[ms] += a * (x - h0)
    return x

def coord_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, random=True, nb=32):
    h = project(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestd = c5(h, n)
    c = corr(h)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        if random and rng is not None:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            order = np.arange(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
                continue
            x = coord_step(c, h, p, n, nb)
            if abs(x - h0) > 1e-13:
                h[p] = x
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        h = project(h, n)
        val = c5(h, n)
        if val < bestd:
            best = h.copy()
            bestd = val
        else:
            h = best.copy()
        c = corr(h)
    h = best.copy()
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    val = c5(h, n)
    return h, min(bestd, val)

def local_opt(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestd = c5(h, n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 15:
        pend = min(deadline, time.time() + 0.4 * rem)
        h2, v2 = try_pairs(h, n, pend, rng, grid=33)
        h2 = project(h2, n)
        v2 = c5(h2, n)
        if v2 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h2, v2
    if time.time() + 5 < deadline:
        h3 = best.copy()
        h3, v3 = coord_descent(h3, n, 220, deadline, rng, True, 32)
        if v3 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h3, v3
    if time.time() + 10 < deadline:
        h4 = best.copy()
        h4, v4 = try_pairs(h4, n, deadline - 2, rng, grid=49)
        h4 = project(h4, n)
        v4 = c5(h4, n)
        if v4 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h4, v4
    h = best.copy()
    h = fix_sum(h, n)
    v = c5(h, n)
    return h, min(bestd, v)

def make_candidate(base, n, rng, typ):
    h = base.copy()
    if typ == 0:
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
        f -= f.mean()
        h += 0.05 * f
    elif typ == 1:
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n)
        f = np.sin(np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
        f += 0.5 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
        f -= f.mean()
        h += 0.08 * f
    elif typ == 2:
        b = (rng.rand(n) < 0.5).astype(float)
        h = 0.85 * base + 0.15 * b
    else:
        hh = 0.5 * (base + 1.0 - base[::-1])
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
        f -= f.mean()
        h = 0.9 * hh + 0.03 * f
    return project(h, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 2.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.1 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        h0 = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n0 < 30:
        n0 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0, 2, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.1 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        h0 = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    h0 = project(h0, n0)
    best = h0.copy()
    bestd = c5(h0, n0)

    parent_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(25.0, 0.45 * float(budget_s)))
    h1, v1 = local_opt(best, n0, parent_end, rng)
    if v1 < bestd:
        best, bestd = h1, v1

    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
        for typ in (0, 1, 2, 3):
            if time.time() > deadline - 90:
                break
            rem = deadline - 90 - time.time()
            if rem < 25:
                break
            alloc = min(80.0, max(20.0, rem / 4.0))
            td = min(deadline - 90, time.time() + alloc)
            if td <= time.time() + 4:
                break
            cand = make_candidate(best, n0, rng, typ)
            h2, v2 = local_opt(cand, n0, td, rng)
            if v2 < bestd:
                best, bestd = h2, v2

    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        h3, v3 = local_opt(best, n0, deadline, rng)
        if v3 < bestd:
            best, bestd = h3, v3

    best = fix_sum(best, n0)
    bestd = c5(best, n0)
    return best, float(bestd), int(n0)
```
